- **waves**: WAVES (Workers, Aggregate, Verify, Extend): wave-based orchestration for Cursor. Decompose a big task into independent slices, fan them out to isolated parallel subagents with the Task tool as a bounded wave, verify each handoff, then synthesize. The manifest is the stop function: continue until every slice is terminal; stop only on completion, stagnation, or budget. Reduce entropy first; route scouting to cheap fast models. Runs in place of cloud orchestration. Opt-in via /waves. For large research, analysis, audits, and codebase or data exploration. Formerly parallel-orchestrate. ([RayFernando1337/rayfernando-skills](https://github.com/RayFernando1337/rayfernando-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/waves))
- **waves**: Work framework: workspace init (scoping interview → CLAUDE.md + master plan + skill routing), wave-based parallel execution on agent teams or subagents, ≤4h task breakdown with exit checks, mandatory decision briefs on every real fork, coordinator status blurbs, fresh-docs discipline for third-party dashboards. ([asunalabs/claude-skills](https://github.com/asunalabs/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/waves))
- **waves**: Product Consciousness Framework for AI agents. Supports software, general, and agentic project types. Includes governing_principles as the universal anchor of the organizational governance chain (company_blueprint → ecosystem rules → local rules → logbooks), 4 defense layers (inline rules + banner + post-impl audit + integrity audit), adversarial subagents (orthogonality reviewer + integrity reviewer + verifier pattern A→B), background metacognition, and multi-session continuity. Complete product lifecycle from feasibility analysis (Monte Carlo / Bayesian) to resolution. ([exovian-developments/waves-cowork-plugin](https://github.com/exovian-developments/waves-cowork-plugin))
